9: pub fn render(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, title: &str, buffer: &str) {
10:     let popup = centered_rect(40, 4, area);
11:     f.render_widget(Clear, popup);
12: 
13:     let block = Block::default()
14:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
15:         .border_type(BorderType::Rounded)
16:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(Color::Blue))

20:     let text = Line::from(vec![

22:         Span::styled(cursor, Style::default().fg(Color::Blue)),
23:     ]);
24: 
25:     let paragraph = Paragraph::new(text).block(block);
26:     f.render_widget(paragraph, popup);
27: }
28: 
29: fn centered_rect(width: u16, height: u16, area: Rect) -> Rect {
30:     let vertical = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(height)]).flex(Flex::Center);
31:     let horizontal = Layout::horizontal([Constraint::Length(width)]).flex(Flex::Center);
32:     let [vert] = vertical.areas(area);
33:     let [rect] = horizontal.areas(vert);
34:     rect
35: }

52: impl FieldSelection {
53:     /// Build the visible field list for a given component.
54:     pub fn fields_for(comp: &crate::config::types::ComponentConfig) -> Vec<FieldSelection> {
55:         let mut fields = vec![Self::Enabled, Self::StyleMode];
56: 
57:         if comp.id == ComponentId::Model {
58:             let pm_enabled = comp.icon.per_model.as_ref().is_some_and(|pm| pm.enabled);
59:             if pm_enabled {
60:                 fields.push(Self::PerModelIcons);
61:                 fields.push(Self::EffortLevel);
62:                 fields.push(Self::ThinkingIcon);
63:                 fields.push(Self::ModelSearch);
64:                 fields.push(Self::ModelReplace);
65:                 fields.push(Self::FlashIcon);
66:                 fields.push(Self::ProIcon);
67:                 fields.push(Self::UltraIcon);
68:                 fields.push(Self::FlashLiteIcon);
69:                 fields.push(Self::OpusIcon);
70:                 fields.push(Self::SonnetIcon);
71:                 fields.push(Self::HaikuIcon);
72:                 fields.push(Self::FableIcon);
73:                 fields.push(Self::MythosIcon);
74:             } else {
75:                 fields.push(Self::PlainIcon);
76:                 fields.push(Self::NerdFontIcon);
77:                 fields.push(Self::PerModelIcons);
78:                 fields.push(Self::EffortLevel);
79:                 fields.push(Self::ThinkingIcon);
80:                 fields.push(Self::ModelSearch);
81:                 fields.push(Self::ModelReplace);
82:             }
83:         } else {
84:             fields.push(Self::PlainIcon);
85:             fields.push(Self::NerdFontIcon);
86:             if comp.id == ComponentId::Hostname {
87:                 fields.push(Self::HostnameRstrip);
88:             }
89:             if comp.id == ComponentId::Worktree {
90:                 fields.push(Self::WorktreeOutside);
91:                 fields.push(Self::WorktreeOriginalBranch);
92:             }
93:             if comp.id == ComponentId::Git {
94:                 fields.push(Self::GitAutohideBranch);
95:             }
96:             if comp.id == ComponentId::PullRequest {
97:                 fields.extend([Self::PrReviewState, Self::PrUrl, Self::PrOscHyperlinks]);
98:             }
99:             if matches!(
100:                 comp.id,
101:                 ComponentId::UsageFiveHour | ComponentId::UsageSevenDay
102:             ) {
103:                 fields.push(Self::UsageValue);
104:             }
105:         }
106: 
107:         fields.extend([
108:             Self::IconColor,
109:             Self::TextColor,
110:             Self::BackgroundColor,
111:             Self::Bold,
112:         ]);
113:         fields
114:     }
115: 
116:     /// Legacy fixed count for non-model components.
117:     pub fn count() -> usize {
118:         8
119:     }
120: 
121:     /// Legacy index lookup (for non-model components).

123:         match i {
124:             0 => Self::Enabled,

323:                 FieldSelection::FlashLiteIcon => (
324:                     "Flash Lite Icon",
325:                     pm.map_or(String::new(), |p| {
326:                         p.flash_lite.for_mode(theme.style.mode).to_string()
327:                     }),
328:                     None,
329:                 ),
330:                 FieldSelection::OpusIcon => (
331:                     "Opus Icon",
332:                     pm.map_or(String::new(), |p| {
333:                         p.opus.for_mode(theme.style.mode).to_string()
334:                     }),
335:                     None,
336:                 ),
337:                 FieldSelection::FableIcon => (
338:                     "Fable Icon",
339:                     pm.map_or(String::new(), |p| {
340:                         p.fable.for_mode(theme.style.mode).to_string()
341:                     }),
342:                     None,
343:                 ),
344:                 FieldSelection::MythosIcon => (
345:                     "Mythos Icon",
346:                     pm.map_or(String::new(), |p| {
347:                         p.mythos.for_mode(theme.style.mode).to_string()
348:                     }),
349:                     None,
350:                 ),
351:                 FieldSelection::SonnetIcon => (
352:                     "Sonnet Icon",
353:                     pm.map_or(String::new(), |p| {
354:                         p.sonnet.for_mode(theme.style.mode).to_string()
355:                     }),
356:                     None,
357:                 ),
358:                 FieldSelection::HaikuIcon => (
359:                     "Haiku Icon",
360:                     pm.map_or(String::new(), |p| {
361:                         p.haiku.for_mode(theme.style.mode).to_string()
362:                     }),
363:                     None,
364:                 ),
365:                 FieldSelection::IconColor => (
366:                     "Icon Color",
367:                     format_color(comp.colors.icon.as_ref()),
368:                     swatch_color(comp.colors.icon.as_ref()),
369:                 ),
370:                 FieldSelection::TextColor => (
371:                     "Text Color",
372:                     format_color(comp.colors.text.as_ref()),
373:                     swatch_color(comp.colors.text.as_ref()),
374:                 ),
375:                 FieldSelection::BackgroundColor => (
376:                     "Bg Color",
377:                     format_color(comp.colors.background.as_ref()),
378:                     swatch_color(comp.colors.background.as_ref()),
379:                 ),
380:                 FieldSelection::Bold => (
381:                     "Bold",
382:                     if comp.styles.text_bold { "Yes" } else { "No" }.into(),
383:                     None,
384:                 ),
385:             })
386:             .collect();
387: 
388:         let label_col_width = field_data
389:             .iter()
390:             .map(|(l, _, _)| l.len())
391:             .max()
392:             .unwrap_or(0)
393:             + 1;
394: 
395:         let all_items: Vec<ListItem> = field_data
396:             .iter()
397:             .enumerate()
398:             .map(|(i, (label, value, swatch))| {
399:                 let field = visible_fields[i];
400:                 let is_selected = field == selected_field && is_focused;
401: 
402:                 let style = if is_selected {
403:                     Style::default()
404:                         .fg(Color::Yellow)
405:                         .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
406:                 } else {
407:                     Style::default().fg(Color::Gray)
408:                 };
409: 
410:                 let cursor = if is_selected { "> " } else { "  " };
411: 
412:                 let mut spans = vec![
413:                     Span::styled(cursor, style),
414:                     Span::styled(
415:                         format!(
416:                             "{:<width$} ",
417:                             format!("{}:", label),
418:                             width = label_col_width
419:                         ),
420:                         style,
421:                     ),
422:                     Span::styled(value.clone(), Style::default().fg(Color::White)),
423:                 ];
424: 
425:                 if let Some(color) = swatch {
426:                     spans.push(Span::raw(" "));
427:                     spans.push(Span::styled(
428:                         "\u{2588}\u{2588}",
429:                         Style::default().fg(*color),
430:                     ));
431:                 }
432: 
433:                 ListItem::new(Line::from(spans))
434:             })
435:             .collect();
436: 
437:         let total = all_items.len();
438:         let selected_idx = visible_fields
439:             .iter()
440:             .position(|f| *f == selected_field)
441:             .unwrap_or(0);
442:         let inner_height = area.height.saturating_sub(2) as usize; // borders
443: 
444:         let items = if total <= inner_height {
445:             all_items
446:         } else {
447:             // Both arrows always shown; visible slots = inner_height - 2
448:             let visible = inner_height.saturating_sub(2);
449:             let half = visible / 2;
450:             let raw_offset = selected_idx.saturating_sub(half);
451:             let max_offset = total.saturating_sub(visible);
452:             let offset = raw_offset.min(max_offset);
453: 
454:             let has_above = offset > 0;
455:             let has_below = offset + visible < total;
456:             let arrow_active = Style::default().fg(Color::Gray);
457:             let arrow_inactive = Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray);
458: 
459:             let mut visible_items: Vec<ListItem> = Vec::new();
460:             visible_items.push(ListItem::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
461:                 " \u{2bac}",
462:                 if has_above {
463:                     arrow_active
464:                 } else {
465:                     arrow_inactive
466:                 },
467:             ))));
468:             visible_items.extend(all_items.into_iter().skip(offset).take(visible));
469:             visible_items.push(ListItem::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
470:                 " \u{2bae}",
471:                 if has_below {
472:                     arrow_active
473:                 } else {
474:                     arrow_inactive
475:                 },
476:             ))));
477:             visible_items
478:         };
479: 
480:         let border_style = if is_focused {
481:             Style::default().fg(Color::Blue)
482:         } else {
483:             Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray)
484:         };
485: 
486:         let title = Line::from(vec![
487:             Span::styled(format!(" {} ", comp.display_name()), border_style),
488:             Span::styled(
489:                 format!("- {} ", comp.id.description()),
490:                 Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray),
491:             ),
492:         ]);
493:         let block = Block::default()
494:             .borders(Borders::ALL)
495:             .border_type(BorderType::Rounded)
496:             .border_style(border_style)
497:             .title(title);
498: 
499:         let list = List::new(items).block(block);
500:         f.render_widget(list, area);
501:     }
502: }
503: 
504: fn format_color(color: Option<&AnsiColor>) -> String {
505:     match color {
506:         Some(c) => c.to_string(),
507:         None => "\u{2014}".into(), // —
508:     }
509: }
510: 
511: fn swatch_color(color: Option<&AnsiColor>) -> Option<Color> {
512:     color.map(render::ansi_to_ratatui_color)
513: }

10: pub fn render(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, state: &ColorPickerState) {
11:     let popup = centered_rect(52, 14, area);
12:     f.render_widget(Clear, popup);
13: 
14:     let mut items: Vec<ListItem> = Vec::new();
15: 
16:     // Mode tabs
17:     let modes = [
18:         ("16-Color", ColorPickerMode::Color16),
19:         ("256-Color", ColorPickerMode::Color256),
20:         ("RGB", ColorPickerMode::Rgb),
21:     ];
22:     let mode_spans: Vec<Span> = modes
23:         .iter()
24:         .map(|(label, mode)| {
25:             if state.mode == *mode {
26:                 Span::styled(
27:                     format!("[{}]", label),
28:                     Style::default()
29:                         .fg(Color::Blue)
30:                         .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
31:                 )
32:             } else {
33:                 Span::styled(format!(" {} ", label), Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray))
34:             }
35:         })
36:         .collect();
37:     items.push(ListItem::new(Line::from(mode_spans)));
38:     items.push(ListItem::new(Line::from("")));
39: 
40:     match *state.mode.current() {
41:         ColorPickerMode::Color16 => {
42:             // Two columns: 0-7 left, 8-15 right
43:             for row in 0u8..8 {
44:                 let left = row;
45:                 let right = row + 8;
46:                 let mut spans = Vec::new();
47: 
48:                 // Left column
49:                 let is_sel_l = left == state.c16_selection;
50:                 let cursor_l = if is_sel_l { "> " } else { "  " };
51:                 let color_l = c16_to_color(left);
52:                 spans.push(Span::styled(
53:                     cursor_l,
54:                     if is_sel_l {
55:                         Style::default().fg(Color::Yellow)
56:                     } else {
57:                         Style::default()
58:                     },
59:                 ));
60:                 spans.push(Span::styled(
61:                     "\u{2588}\u{2588}",
62:                     Style::default().fg(color_l),
63:                 ));
64:                 spans.push(Span::styled(
65:                     format!(" {:3}", left),
66:                     if is_sel_l {
67:                         Style::default()
68:                             .fg(Color::Yellow)
69:                             .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
70:                     } else {
71:                         Style::default().fg(Color::White)
72:                     },
73:                 ));
74: 
75:                 // Gap
76:                 spans.push(Span::raw("  "));
77: 
78:                 // Right column

11: /// Total number of selectable (non-header) entries across all sections.
12: pub fn selectable_count(sections: &[SectionView<'_>]) -> usize {
13:     sections.iter().map(|s| s.entries.len()).sum()
14: }
15: 
16: /// Total number of flat rows (1 header + N entries per section).
17: pub fn flat_len(sections: &[SectionView<'_>]) -> usize {
18:     sections.iter().map(|s| s.entries.len() + 1).sum()
19: }
20: 
21: /// Map a selectable index → flat row index.
22: pub fn selectable_to_flat(sections: &[SectionView<'_>], sel: usize) -> Option<usize> {
23:     let mut flat = 0;
24:     let mut remaining = sel;
25:     for s in sections {
26:         flat += 1; // header
27:         let len = s.entries.len();
28:         if remaining < len {
29:             return Some(flat + remaining);
30:         }
31:         remaining -= len;
32:         flat += len;
33:     }
34:     None
35: }
36: 
37: /// Look up the IconEntry at a given selectable index.
38: pub fn entry_at_selectable<'a>(
39:     sections: &'a [SectionView<'a>],
40:     sel: usize,
41: ) -> Option<&'a IconEntry> {
42:     let mut remaining = sel;
43:     for s in sections {
44:         let len = s.entries.len();
45:         if remaining < len {
46:             return s.entries.get(remaining);
47:         }
48:         remaining -= len;
49:     }
50:     None
51: }

179: /// Render a text field with a block cursor at the given character position.
180: fn render_text_with_cursor(text: &str, cursor_pos: usize) -> Line<'static> {
181:     let before: String = text.chars().take(cursor_pos).collect();
182:     let cursor_char: String = text.chars().nth(cursor_pos).map_or(
183:         "\u{2588}".to_string(), // block cursor when at end
184:         |c| c.to_string(),
185:     );
186:     let after: String = text.chars().skip(cursor_pos + 1).collect();
187: 
188:     let cursor_style = if cursor_pos < text.chars().count() {
189:         // Cursor is on a character — highlight it
190:         Style::default().fg(Color::Black).bg(Color::Blue)
191:     } else {
192:         // Cursor is at end — show block
193:         Style::default().fg(Color::Blue)
194:     };
195: 
196:     Line::from(vec![
197:         Span::raw(" "),
198:         Span::styled(before, Style::default().fg(Color::White)),
199:         Span::styled(cursor_char, cursor_style),
200:         Span::styled(after, Style::default().fg(Color::White)),
201:     ])
202: }

306:             self.should_quit = true;

425:     fn move_selection(&mut self, delta: i32) {
426:         match *self.selected_panel.current() {
427:             Panel::ComponentList => {
428:                 let count = self.component_count();
429:                 if count > 0 {
430:                     self.selected_component =
431:                         (self.selected_component as i32 + delta).rem_euclid(count as i32) as usize;
432:                 }
433:                 // Clamp field selection to valid fields for the new component
434:                 if let Some(comp) = self.theme.components.get(self.selected_component) {
435:                     let fields = FieldSelection::fields_for(comp);
436:                     if !fields.contains(&self.selected_field) {
437:                         self.selected_field = FieldSelection::Enabled;
438:                     }
439:                 }
440:             }
441:             Panel::Editor => {
442:                 if let Some(comp) = self.theme.components.get(self.selected_component) {
443:                     let fields = FieldSelection::fields_for(comp);
444:                     let current = fields
445:                         .iter()
446:                         .position(|f| *f == self.selected_field)
447:                         .unwrap_or(0) as i32;
448:                     let new_idx = (current + delta).clamp(0, fields.len() as i32 - 1) as usize;
449:                     self.selected_field = fields[new_idx];
450:                 }
451:             }
452:         }
453:     }
454: 
455:     fn toggle_current(&mut self) {
456:         match *self.selected_panel.current() {
457:             Panel::ComponentList => {
458:                 if let Some(comp) = self.theme.components.get_mut(self.selected_component) {
459:                     comp.enabled = !comp.enabled;
460:                     self.status_message = Some(format!(
461:                         "{} {}",
462:                         comp.id.display_name(),
463:                         if comp.enabled { "enabled" } else { "disabled" }
464:                     ));
465:                     self.mark_dirty();

1296:     fn handle_color_picker(&mut self, code: KeyCode, _modifiers: KeyModifiers, is_cancel: bool) {
1297:         if is_cancel {
1298:             self.color_picker_open = false;
1299:             return;
1300:         }
1301:         match code {
1302:             KeyCode::Tab => {
1303:                 self.color_picker.mode.move_next();
1304:             }
1305:             KeyCode::BackTab => {
1306:                 self.color_picker.mode.move_prev();
1307:             }
1308:             KeyCode::Up => match *self.color_picker.mode.current() {
1309:                 ColorPickerMode::Color16 => {
1310:                     let sel = self.color_picker.c16_selection;
1311:                     if !sel.is_multiple_of(8) {
1312:                         self.color_picker.c16_selection = sel - 1;
1313:                     }
1314:                 }
1315:                 ColorPickerMode::Color256 => {
1316:                     self.color_picker.c256_selection =
1317:                         self.color_picker.c256_selection.saturating_sub(1);
1318:                 }
1319:                 ColorPickerMode::Rgb => {
1320:                     if self.color_picker.rgb_focus > 0 {
1321:                         self.color_picker.rgb_focus -= 1;
1322:                     }
1323:                 }
1324:             },
1325:             KeyCode::Down => match *self.color_picker.mode.current() {
1326:                 ColorPickerMode::Color16 => {
1327:                     let sel = self.color_picker.c16_selection;
1328:                     if sel % 8 < 7 {
1329:                         self.color_picker.c16_selection = sel + 1;
1330:                     }
1331:                 }
1332:                 ColorPickerMode::Color256 => {
1333:                     self.color_picker.c256_selection =
1334:                         self.color_picker.c256_selection.saturating_add(1);
1335:                 }
1336:                 ColorPickerMode::Rgb => {
1337:                     if self.color_picker.rgb_focus < 2 {
1338:                         self.color_picker.rgb_focus += 1;
1339:                     }
1340:                 }
1341:             },
1342:             KeyCode::Left => match *self.color_picker.mode.current() {
1343:                 ColorPickerMode::Color16 => {
1344:                     if self.color_picker.c16_selection >= 8 {
1345:                         self.color_picker.c16_selection -= 8;
1346:                     }
1347:                 }
1348:                 ColorPickerMode::Color256 => {
1349:                     self.color_picker.c256_selection =
1350:                         self.color_picker.c256_selection.saturating_sub(16);
1351:                 }
1352:                 _ => {}
1353:             },
1354:             KeyCode::Right => match *self.color_picker.mode.current() {
1355:                 ColorPickerMode::Color16 => {
1356:                     if self.color_picker.c16_selection < 8 {
1357:                         self.color_picker.c16_selection += 8;
1358:                     }
1359:                 }
1360:                 ColorPickerMode::Color256 => {
1361:                     self.color_picker.c256_selection =
1362:                         self.color_picker.c256_selection.saturating_add(16);
1363:                 }
1364:                 _ => {}
1365:             },
1366:             KeyCode::Char(c)
1367:                 if c.is_ascii_digit() && self.color_picker.mode == ColorPickerMode::Rgb =>
1368:             {
1369:                 let field = match self.color_picker.rgb_focus {
1370:                     0 => &mut self.color_picker.rgb_r,
1371:                     1 => &mut self.color_picker.rgb_g,
1372:                     _ => &mut self.color_picker.rgb_b,
1373:                 };
1374:                 if field.len() < 3 {
1375:                     field.push(c);
1376:                 }
1377:             }
1378:             KeyCode::Backspace if self.color_picker.mode == ColorPickerMode::Rgb => {
1379:                 let field = match self.color_picker.rgb_focus {
1380:                     0 => &mut self.color_picker.rgb_r,
1381:                     1 => &mut self.color_picker.rgb_g,
1382:                     _ => &mut self.color_picker.rgb_b,
1383:                 };
1384:                 field.pop();
1385:             }
1386:             KeyCode::Char('x') | KeyCode::Char('X') => {
1387:                 // Remove color (set to None)
1388:                 if let Some(comp) = self.theme.components.get_mut(self.selected_component) {
1389:                     match self.selected_field {
1390:                         FieldSelection::IconColor => comp.colors.icon = None,
1391:                         FieldSelection::TextColor => comp.colors.text = None,
1392:                         FieldSelection::BackgroundColor => comp.colors.background = None,
1393:                         _ => {}
1394:                     }
1395:                     self.mark_dirty();

The search for ""ratatui-textarea" docs rs" returned the following summary:
You can find the documentation for `ratatui-textarea` on **Docs.rs**[1].

*   **Docs.rs Link:** [https://docs.rs/ratatui-textarea](https://docs.rs/ratatui-textarea) (or search for "ratatui-textarea" directly on [docs.rs](https://docs.rs))[1].

### Overview
`ratatui-textarea` is a popular widget for the [Ratatui](https://ratatui.rs/) library that provides a powerful, multi-line text editor similar to an HTML `<textarea>`[2][3].

**Key Features:**
*   **Editor Operations:** Supports inserting/deleting characters, undo/redo, auto-scrolling, text selection, and yank support[2][3].
*   **Navigation:** Includes Emacs-like shortcuts (e.g., `C-n`, `C-p`, `C-a`, `C-e`) and mouse scrolling[2][3].
*   **Customization:** Offers features like line numbers, cursor line highlighting, soft wrapping (character/word), and regex search[2][3].
*   **Backend Agnostic:** Works with `crossterm`, `termion`, `termwiz`, and custom backends[2][3].

### Getting Started
You can explore usage examples directly in the crate's repository[2][3], which cover common scenarios such as:
*   **Minimal:** A basic setup with `crossterm`[2][3].
*   **Editor:** A simple multi-file editor example[2][3].
*   **Vim:** An example of implementing Vim-like modal editing[2][3].
*   **Split/Popup:** Examples of using multiple textareas or popup-style inputs[2][3].

*Note: If you are looking for a version maintained with newer Ratatui compatibility (Ratatui 0.30+), there is also a community-maintained fork called `tui-textarea`[4].*

To use `ratatui-textarea` in a project utilizing Ratatui, you should add the latest version of the crate to your `Cargo.toml`. As of mid-2026, `ratatui-textarea` is actively maintained with versions like `0.9.2` available, which are compatible with modern Ratatui releases[1][2][3].

### Adding to your project
You can add it to your project by running the following command in your terminal:
```bash
cargo add ratatui-textarea
```
Or, add this line to your `Cargo.toml` dependencies:
```toml
[dependencies]
ratatui = "0.29" # or the version you are using
ratatui-textarea = "0.9.2"
```

### Usage Tips
*   **Version Matching:** Ensure that the version of `ratatui-textarea` you choose is compatible with the Ratatui version in your `Cargo.toml`. If you encounter issues with newer Ratatui versions (e.g., `0.30+`), community forks such as `tui-textarea-2` are also available and maintained to keep up with the latest Ratatui updates[4][5][6].
*   **Documentation & Examples:** You can find extensive examples in the [official GitHub repository](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui-textarea), which demonstrate how to implement multi-line editing, Vim-like modes, and split-screen setups[7].
*   **Initialization:** In your code, you typically initialize a `TextArea` and update it by passing crossterm events (or other backend events) to it[7][8].

```rust
use ratatui_textarea::TextArea;

// Inside your application state
let mut textarea = TextArea::default();

// In your event loop, process events:
// textarea.input(event);

// In your draw function, render the widget:
// frame.render_widget(textarea.widget(), area);
```

For specific feature requirements (like search or additional styling), check the `Cargo.toml` feature flags for the version you have installed[7][6].

9: 
10: pub fn render(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, themes: &[(String, PathBuf)], selection: usize) {
11:     let mut items: Vec<ListItem> = Vec::new();
12: 
13:     for (i, (name, _)) in themes.iter().enumerate() {
14:         let style = if i == selection {
15:             Style::default()
16:                 .fg(Color::Yellow)
17:                 .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
18:         } else {
19:             Style::default().fg(Color::White)
20:         };
21:         let cursor = if i == selection { "> " } else { "  " };
22:         items.push(ListItem::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
23:             format!("{}{}", cursor, name),
24:             style,
25:         ))));
26:     }
27: 
28:     let height = (items.len() as u16 + 2).min(area.height.saturating_sub(4));
29:     let popup = centered_rect(40, height, area);
30:     f.render_widget(Clear, popup);
31: 
32:     let block = Block::default()
33:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
34:         .border_type(BorderType::Rounded)
35:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(Color::Blue))
36:         .title(" Open Theme ");
37: 
38:     let list = List::new(items).block(block);
39:     f.render_widget(list, popup);
40: }
41: 
42: fn centered_rect(width: u16, height: u16, area: Rect) -> Rect {
43:     let vertical = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(height)]).flex(Flex::Center);
44:     let horizontal = Layout::horizontal([Constraint::Length(width)]).flex(Flex::Center);
45:     let [vert] = vertical.areas(area);
46:     let [rect] = horizontal.areas(vert);
47:     rect
48: }

1: use crate::config::manager;
2: use crate::config::theme::UserTheme;
3: use crate::config::types::StyleMode;
4: use crate::core::render;
5: use ratatui::{
6:     Frame,
7:     layout::{Constraint, Flex, Layout, Rect},
8:     style::{Color, Modifier, Style},
9:     text::{Line, Span},
10:     widgets::{Block, BorderType, Borders, Clear, List, ListItem},
11: };
12: 
13: pub fn render_colors(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, selection: usize, theme: &UserTheme) {
14:     let user_themes = manager::list_themes().unwrap_or_default();
15:     let user_theme_data = load_user_theme_data(&user_themes);
16:     let schemes = filter_color_schemes(&user_theme_data);
17:     let texts = render::demo_texts_compact();
18: 
19:     // Pad names to max width
20:     let max_name_len = schemes
21:         .iter()
22:         .map(|s| s.name.len())
23:         .chain(user_themes.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.len()))
24:         .max()
25:         .unwrap_or(0);
26: 
27:     // Build render lines for presets
28:     let mut render_lines: Vec<render::RenderLine> = schemes
29:         .iter()
30:         .map(|scheme| {
31:             let mut preview_theme = theme.clone();
32:             scheme.apply_to(&mut preview_theme.components);
33:             let mode = if scheme.is_powerline() {
34:                 if let Some(pl_icons) = crate::presets::icon_sets::find("Powerline") {
35:                     pl_icons.apply_to(&mut preview_theme.components);
36:                 }
37:                 StyleMode::Powerline
38:             } else {
39:                 preview_theme.style.mode
40:             };
41:             render::build_render_line(&preview_theme.components, mode, &texts)
42:         })
43:         .collect();
44: 
45:     // Build render lines for user themes (apply their colors to current theme's icons)
46:     let mut user_render_lines: Vec<render::RenderLine> = user_theme_data
47:         .iter()
48:         .map(|ut| {
49:             let mut preview_theme = theme.clone();
50:             for src in &ut.components {
51:                 if let Some(dest) = preview_theme.components.iter_mut().find(|c| c.id == src.id) {
52:                     dest.colors = src.colors.clone();
53:                     dest.styles = src.styles.clone();
54:                 }
55:             }
56:             render::build_render_line(&preview_theme.components, preview_theme.style.mode, &texts)
57:         })
58:         .collect();
59: 
60:     // Align all lines together
61:     let mut all_lines: Vec<&mut render::RenderLine> = render_lines
62:         .iter_mut()
63:         .chain(user_render_lines.iter_mut())
64:         .collect();
65:     render::align_lines_refs(&mut all_lines);
66: 
67:     let mut items: Vec<ListItem> = Vec::new();
68:

129: pub fn render_icons(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, selection: usize, theme: &UserTheme) {
130:     let user_themes = manager::list_themes().unwrap_or_default();
131:     let user_theme_data = load_user_theme_data(&user_themes);
132:     let icon_sets = filter_icon_sets(&user_theme_data);
133:     let texts = render::demo_texts_compact();
134: 
135:     // Pad names to max width (all ASCII, so len() == display width)
136:     let max_name_len = icon_sets
137:         .iter()
138:         .map(|s| s.name.len())
139:         .chain(user_themes.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.len()))
140:         .max()
141:         .unwrap_or(0);
142: 
143:     // Build render lines for presets
144:     let mut render_lines: Vec<render::RenderLine> = icon_sets
145:         .iter()
146:         .map(|set| {
147:             let mut preview_theme = theme.clone();
148:             set.apply_to(&mut preview_theme.components);
149:             let mode = if set.is_powerline() {
150:                 if let Some(pl) = crate::presets::color_schemes::find("Powerline Dark") {
151:                     pl.apply_to(&mut preview_theme.components);
152:                 }
153:                 StyleMode::Powerline
154:             } else {
155:                 preview_theme.style.mode
156:             };
157:             render::build_render_line(&preview_theme.components, mode, &texts)
158:         })
159:         .collect();
160: 
161:     // Build render lines for user themes (apply their icons to current theme's colors)
162:     let mut user_render_lines: Vec<render::RenderLine> = user_theme_data
163:         .iter()
164:         .map(|ut| {
165:             let mut preview_theme = theme.clone();
166:             for src in &ut.components {
167:                 if let Some(dest) = preview_theme.components.iter_mut().find(|c| c.id == src.id) {
168:                     dest.icon = src.icon.clone();
169:                 }
170:             }
171:             let mode = if matches!(
172:                 ut.style.mode,
173:                 StyleMode::Powerline | StyleMode::PlainPowerline
174:             ) {
175:                 if let Some(pl) = crate::presets::color_schemes::find("Powerline Dark") {
176:                     pl.apply_to(&mut preview_theme.components);
177:                 }
178:                 StyleMode::Powerline
179:             } else {
180:                 preview_theme.style.mode
181:             };
182:             render::build_render_line(&preview_theme.components, mode, &texts)
183:         })
184:         .collect();
185: 
186:     // Align all lines together
187:     let mut all_lines: Vec<&mut render::RenderLine> = render_lines
188:         .iter_mut()
189:         .chain(user_render_lines.iter_mut())
190:         .collect();
191:     render::align_lines_refs(&mut all_lines);
192: 
193:     let mut items: Vec<ListItem> = Vec::new();
194: 
195:     for (i, (set, line)) in icon_sets.iter().zip(render_lines.iter()).enumerate() {

197: 
198:         let cursor = if i == selection { "> " } else { "  " };

200:         let padded_name = format!("{:<width$} ", set.name, width = max_name_len);

202:         let mut spans = vec![
203:             Span::styled(cursor.to_string(), cursor_style),

259: fn item_style(index: usize, selection: usize) -> Style {
260:     if index == selection {
261:         Style::default()
262:             .fg(Color::Yellow)
263:             .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
264:     } else {
265:         Style::default().fg(Color::White)
266:     }
267: }
268: 
269: fn centered_rect(width: u16, height: u16, area: Rect) -> Rect {
270:     let vertical = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(height)]).flex(Flex::Center);
271:     let horizontal = Layout::horizontal([Constraint::Length(width)]).flex(Flex::Center);
272:     let [vert] = vertical.areas(area);
273:     let [rect] = horizontal.areas(vert);
274:     rect
275: }
276: 
277: fn load_user_theme_data(user_themes: &[(String, std::path::PathBuf)]) -> Vec<UserTheme> {
278:     user_themes
279:         .iter()
280:         .filter_map(|(_, path)| manager::load_theme(path).ok())
281:         .collect()
282: }
283: 
284: /// Color scheme presets not already supplied by any user theme.
285: pub fn filter_color_schemes(
286:     user_theme_data: &[UserTheme],
287: ) -> Vec<crate::presets::color_schemes::ColorScheme> {

291:             !user_theme_data
292:                 .iter()
293:                 .any(|t| s.is_supplied_by(&t.components))
294:         })
295:         .collect()
296: }

Search ratatui_textarea input handling

tool_result(search_web) 2026-08-19 04:15:30
Created At: 2026-08-18T22:15:30-06:00
Completed At: 2026-08-18T22:15:33-06:00
The search for ""ratatui_textarea::TextArea" input keyevent crossterm" returned the following summary:
To handle `crossterm` key events with `ratatui-textarea`, you can pass `crossterm`'s `KeyEvent` directly into the `TextArea::input()` method[1][2].

### Basic Implementation

When using the `crossterm` feature (which is enabled by default in `ratatui-textarea`), the `input` method is designed to accept `crossterm::event::KeyEvent` directly[2][3].

```rust
use ratatui_textarea::TextArea;
use crossterm::event::{Event, read, KeyCode};

// 1. Initialize your textarea
let mut textarea = TextArea::default();

// 2. Inside your event loop
if let Event::Key(key_event) = read()? {
    // Check for custom shortcuts first if necessary
    if key_event.code == KeyCode::Esc {
        // Handle your own custom exit logic
    } else {
        // 3. Pass the crossterm KeyEvent directly to textarea
        textarea.input(key_event);
    }
}
```

### Using `ratatui_textarea::Input` for Abstraction
If you need to handle input in a backend-agnostic way or want to perform pattern matching on keys across different potential backends, you can convert the `crossterm` event into the crate's internal `Input` type[4][5].

```rust
use ratatui_textarea::{TextArea, Input, Key};
use crossterm::event::{Event, read};

if let Event::Key(key_event) = read()? {
    // Convert crossterm event to ratatui_textarea::Input
    let input: Input = key_event.into();
    
    // Pattern match to intercept specific keys
    match input {
        Input { key: Key::Char('c'), ctrl: true, .. } => {
            // Handle Ctrl+C
        }
        _ => {
            // Otherwise, let the textarea handle it
            textarea.input(input);
        }
    }
}
```

### Important Notes
*   **Version Compatibility:** Ensure that the version of `crossterm` you are using matches the version expected by `ratatui-textarea`[6]. If you encounter trait bound errors (e.g., `the trait bound Input: From<KeyEvent> is not satisfied`), it is often due to a mismatch between `crossterm` versions (e.g., 0.28 vs 0.29)[7][6].
*   **Custom Shortcuts:** If you want to prevent `textarea` from processing certain keys (like `Enter`), use a `match` statement as shown above to intercept them before calling `textarea.input()`[8][9].
*   **Default Shortcuts:** By default, `textarea.input()` handles many standard editing shortcuts (e.g., Emacs-like bindings such as `Ctrl+A`, `Ctrl+E`, `Ctrl+K`)[3].

197: fn iso_timestamp_zulu() -> String {
198:     let now = std::time::SystemTime::now();
199:     let duration = now
200:         .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
201:         .unwrap_or_default();
202:     let total_secs = duration.as_secs();
203:     let millis = duration.subsec_millis();
204: 
205:     let days = total_secs / 86400;
206:     let rem_secs = total_secs % 86400;
207:     let hours = rem_secs / 3600;
208:     let minutes = (rem_secs % 3600) / 60;
209:     let seconds = rem_secs % 60;
210: 
211:     let z = days as i64 + 719468;
212:     let era = (if z >= 0 { z } else { z - 146096 }) / 146097;
213:     let doe = (z - era * 146097) as u64;
214:     let yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36524 - doe / 146096) / 365;
215:     let y = yoe as i64 + era * 400;
216:     let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
217:     let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
218:     let d = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
219:     let m = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
220:     let year = if m <= 2 { y + 1 } else { y };
221: 
222:     format!(
223:         "{:04}-{:02}-{:02}T{:02}:{:02}:{:02}.{:03}Z",
224:         year, m, d, hours, minutes, seconds, millis
225:     )
226: }
227:

Completed At: 2026-08-18T22:15:41-06:00
The search for ""ratatui_textarea" TextArea single line set_block widget" returned the following summary:
To set a block for a `TextArea` in the `ratatui-textarea` crate, you use the `set_block` method[1][2].

### Setting a Block
You can wrap the `TextArea` in a `ratatui` `Block` to add borders, titles, or padding[1][2]:

```rust
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Borders};
use ratatui_textarea::TextArea;

let mut textarea = TextArea::default();
textarea.set_block(
    Block::default()
        .borders(Borders::ALL)
        .title("Input Field")
);
```

### Implementing Single-Line Behavior
The `ratatui-textarea` widget is designed primarily for multi-line editing[3]. To restrict it to a single line, you should intercept the `Enter` key (and optionally other navigation/shortcut keys that might insert newlines) in your input handling logic[1][2].

When handling input, check if the key is `Enter` and simply do not pass it to `textarea.input()`[1][2]:

```rust
// Example input handling
match input {
    // Prevent Enter from creating a new line
    Input { key: Key::Enter, .. } => {
        // Handle "submit" action here instead
    }
    // Pass other inputs to the textarea
    input => {
        textarea.input(input);
    }
}
```

### Key Considerations
*   **The `single_line` Example:** The crate includes a specific example called `single_line` which demonstrates how to create a single-line input form[3][4]. You can view the source for this in the [ratatui-textarea GitHub repository](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui-textarea) under the `examples/` directory[3][5].
*   **Preventing Newlines:** If you use `textarea.input(input)` for all keys, the widget will naturally allow newlines[6]. You must filter these out before they reach the widget[1].
*   **Layout:** When rendering, ensure the `Rect` you provide to the `TextArea` has a height of `1` (plus any extra space for the `Block` borders if you added them) to visually represent a single-line input[1][2].

9: pub fn render(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, title: &str, buffer: &str) {
10:     let popup = centered_rect(40, 4, area);

12: 
13:     let block = Block::default()

23:     ]);
24: 
25:     let paragraph = Paragraph::new(text).block(block);
26:     f.render_widget(paragraph, popup);
27: }
28: 
29: fn centered_rect(width: u16, height: u16, area: Rect) -> Rect {
30:     let vertical = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(height)]).flex(Flex::Center);
31:     let horizontal = Layout::horizontal([Constraint::Length(width)]).flex(Flex::Center);
32:     let [vert] = vertical.areas(area);
33:     let [rect] = horizontal.areas(vert);
34:     rect
35: }

166: fn render_custom_input(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, state: &IconPickerState) {
167:     let text = render_text_with_cursor(&state.custom_buffer, state.custom_cursor);
168: 
169:     let block = Block::default()
170:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
171:         .border_type(BorderType::Rounded)
172:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(Color::Blue))
173:         .title(Span::styled(" Input ", Style::default().fg(Color::Yellow)));

176:     f.render_widget(para, area);
177: }
178: 
179: /// Render a text field with a block cursor at the given character position.
180: fn render_text_with_cursor(text: &str, cursor_pos: usize) -> Line<'static> {
181:     let before: String = text.chars().take(cursor_pos).collect();
182:     let cursor_char: String = text.chars().nth(cursor_pos).map_or(
183:         "\u{2588}".to_string(), // block cursor when at end
184:         |c| c.to_string(),
185:     );
186:     let after: String = text.chars().skip(cursor_pos + 1).collect();
187: 
188:     let cursor_style = if cursor_pos < text.chars().count() {
189:         // Cursor is on a character — highlight it
190:         Style::default().fg(Color::Black).bg(Color::Blue)
191:     } else {
192:         // Cursor is at end — show block
193:         Style::default().fg(Color::Blue)
194:     };
195: 
196:     Line::from(vec![
197:         Span::raw(" "),
198:         Span::styled(before, Style::default().fg(Color::White)),
199:         Span::styled(cursor_char, cursor_style),
200:         Span::styled(after, Style::default().fg(Color::White)),
201:     ])
202: }
203:

206:     let sections = catalog.sections(tab, &state.search_query);

209:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
210:         .border_type(BorderType::Rounded)

179: /// Render a text field with a block cursor at the given character position.
180: fn render_text_with_cursor(text: &str, cursor_pos: usize) -> Line<'static> {
181:     let before: String = text.chars().take(cursor_pos).collect();
182:     let cursor_char: String = text.chars().nth(cursor_pos).map_or(
183:         "\u{2588}".to_string(), // block cursor when at end
184:         |c| c.to_string(),
185:     );
186:     let after: String = text.chars().skip(cursor_pos + 1).collect();
187: 
188:     let cursor_style = if cursor_pos < text.chars().count() {
189:         // Cursor is on a character — highlight it
190:         Style::default().fg(Color::Black).bg(Color::Blue)
191:     } else {
192:         // Cursor is at end — show block
193:         Style::default().fg(Color::Blue)
194:     };
195: 
196:     Line::from(vec![
197:         Span::raw(" "),
198:         Span::styled(before, Style::default().fg(Color::White)),
199:         Span::styled(cursor_char, cursor_style),
200:         Span::styled(after, Style::default().fg(Color::White)),
201:     ])
202: }

1181:     fn handle_open_menu(&mut self, code: KeyCode, is_cancel: bool) {
1182:         if is_cancel {
1183:             self.open_menu_open = false;
1184:             return;
1185:         }
1186:         match code {
1187:             KeyCode::Up => {
1188:                 if self.open_menu_selection > 0 {
1189:                     self.open_menu_selection -= 1;
1190:                 }
1191:             }
1192:             KeyCode::Down => {
1193:                 if self.open_menu_selection + 1 < self.open_menu_themes.len() {
1194:                     self.open_menu_selection += 1;
1195:                 }
1196:             }
1197:             KeyCode::Enter => {
1198:                 self.open_menu_open = false;
1199:                 let idx = self.open_menu_selection;
1200:                 if let Some((name, path)) = self.open_menu_themes.get(idx).cloned() {
1201:                     if self.is_dirty {
1202:                         // Store which theme to open, then ask to discard
1203:                         self.confirm_dialog_open = true;
1204:                         self.confirm_dialog_message = format!("Discard changes and open {}?", name);
1205:                         self.confirm_dialog_action = ConfirmAction::DiscardAndOpen;
1206:                         // Stash the target in name_input_buffer temporarily
1207:                         self.name_input_buffer = format!("{}\0{}", name, path.display());
1208:                     } else {
1209:                         self.do_open_theme(&name, &path);
1210:                     }
1211:                 }
1212:             }
1213:             _ => {}
1214:         }
1215:     }
1216: 
1217:     fn switch_theme(&mut self, delta: i32) {
1218:         if self.theme_list.is_empty() {
1219:             return;
1220:         }
1221:         let new_idx = (self.theme_list_index as i32 + delta)
1222:             .rem_euclid(self.theme_list.len() as i32) as usize;
1223:         if new_idx == self.theme_list_index {
1224:             return;
1225:         }
1226:         if let Some((name, path)) = self.theme_list.get(new_idx).cloned() {
1227:             if self.is_dirty {
1228:                 // Auto-save before switching
1229:                 self.action_save();
1230:             }
1231:             self.do_open_theme(&name, &path);
1232:             self.theme_list_index = new_idx;
1233:         }
1234:     }

1246:                 self.status_message = Some(format!("Opened {}", name));
1247:             }
1248:             Err(e) => self.status_message = Some(format!("Load error: {}", e)),
1249:         }

1559:                         self.icon_picker.custom_cursor.saturating_sub(1);
1560:                 } else {
1561:                     self.icon_picker.search_cursor =
1562:                         self.icon_picker.search_cursor.saturating_sub(1);
1563:                 }
1564:             }
1565:             KeyCode::Right => {
1566:                 if is_custom {
1567:                     let len = self.icon_picker.custom_buffer.chars().count();
1568:                     if self.icon_picker.custom_cursor < len {
1569:                         self.icon_picker.custom_cursor += 1;
1570:                     }
1571:                 } else {

1573:                     if self.icon_picker.search_cursor < len {
1574:                         self.icon_picker.search_cursor += 1;
1575:                     }
1576:                 }
1577:             }
1578:             KeyCode::Up if !is_custom => {
1579:                 if self.icon_picker.selected_index > 0 {
1580:                     self.icon_picker.selected_index -= 1;
1581:                     self.adjust_icon_picker_scroll();
1582:                 }
1583:             }
1584:             KeyCode::Down if !is_custom => {
1585:                 let max = self.icon_picker_selectable_count();
1586:                 if max > 0 && self.icon_picker.selected_index < max - 1 {
1587:                     self.icon_picker.selected_index += 1;

1590:             }

## 4. Model Knowledge
- **`ratatui_textarea` Details & API**:
  - `TextArea<'static>` implements `Widget` for `&TextArea<'_>`.
  - Single-line styling: `textarea.set_cursor_line_style(Style::default())` avoids underlining the entire line.
  - Border wrapping: `textarea.set_block(block)` wraps the textarea in a Ratatui `Block`.
  - Text extraction: `textarea.lines().first().map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("")`.
  - Initial text & cursor: `let mut ta = TextArea::new(vec![text.to_string()]); ta.move_cursor(CursorMove::End); ta.set_cursor_line_style(Style::default());`.
  - Event feeding: `textarea.input(crossterm::event::KeyEvent::new(code, modifiers))` handles cursor movement, typing, backspace, deletion, and Emacs keybindings automatically.
- **Decoupling DiscardAndOpen Stash**:
  - Previously, `handle_open_menu` stashed `"name\0path"` in `name_input_buffer`. We introduced a dedicated `pub pending_open_theme: Option<(String, PathBuf)>` in `App` to keep `name_input_textarea` purely for text input.

554:         match *self.selected_panel.current() {
555:             Panel::ComponentList => {
556:                 let count = self.component_count();
557:                 if count > 0 {
558:                     self.selected_component =
559:                         (self.selected_component as i32 + delta).rem_euclid(count as i32) as usize;

1190:         if is_cancel {

1192:             return;
1193:         }
1194: 
1195:         let user_themes = manager::list_themes().unwrap_or_default();

1197:             .iter()

1202:

1204:             KeyCode::Up => {
1205:                 if self.import_colors_selection > 0 {
1206:                     self.import_colors_selection -= 1;
1207:                 }
1208:             }
1209:             KeyCode::Down => {
1210:                 if self.import_colors_selection + 1 < total {
1211:                     self.import_colors_selection += 1;
1212:                 }
1213:             }
1214:             KeyCode::Enter => {
1215:                 self.import_colors_open = false;
1216:                 let idx = self.import_colors_selection;
1217:                 if idx < schemes.len() {
1218:                     schemes[idx].apply_to(&mut self.theme.components);
1219:                     self.status_message =
1220:                         Some(format!("Imported colors from {}", schemes[idx].name));
1221:                     self.mark_dirty();
1222:                 } else {

1225:                         && let Ok(src_theme) = manager::load_theme(path)
1226:                     {

1229:                             if let Some(dest) = self
1230:                                 .theme
1231:                                 .components
1232:                                 .iter_mut()
1233:                                 .find(|c| c.id == src_comp.id)
1234:                             {
1235:                                 dest.colors = src_comp.colors.clone();
1236:                                 dest.styles = src_comp.styles.clone();
1237:                             }
1238:                         }
1239:                         self.status_message = Some(format!("Imported colors from {}", name));
1240:                         self.mark_dirty();
1241:                     }
1242:                 }
1243:             }
1244:             _ => {}
1245:         }

*   **`ratatui-textarea`**: This crate is a third-party widget library for `ratatui`[4][5]. As of mid-2026, the official `ratatui-textarea` repository (maintained by the `ratatui` organization) has continued to receive updates to support newer versions of `ratatui`[6].
*   **Version Mismatch**: If you are encountering dependency resolution issues, it is often due to `Cargo` attempting to unify different versions of `ratatui` across your dependency tree[7]. Since `ratatui-textarea` often defines specific compatibility ranges for `ratatui`, you may need to ensure your `Cargo.toml` dependencies align or check for the latest release of `ratatui-textarea` that supports your specific `ratatui` version[7][8].

### Recent Developments (2026)
*   **Ratatui 0.30.0+**: Note that `ratatui` has since moved to 0.30.0 and beyond, which introduced significant architectural changes, such as modularizing the crate into `ratatui-core`, `ratatui-widgets`, etc[9].
*   **Forking/Maintenance**: Because of the rapid evolution of `ratatui`, some users have moved to community-maintained forks (e.g., `tui-textarea-2`) to ensure compatibility with the latest `ratatui` releases (0.30+)[8][10]. If you are building a new project or upgrading, you should check the latest documentation for `ratatui-textarea` to see if it supports your target `ratatui` version[6].

**Recommendation**: If you are strictly required to use `ratatui` 0.29.0, check the specific release tags on the [ratatui-textarea GitHub repository](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui-textarea) to find the version that was active during that time (typically version `0.8.x` or early `0.9.x`)[6][10][11].

### Note on Related Crates
Be aware that there are similarly named crates in the Rust ecosystem:
*   **`tui-textarea`**: Historically, many users utilized `tui-textarea`[1][2], which is a widely used multi-line text editor widget that supports both `ratatui` and `tui-rs`.
*   **`tui-textarea-2`**: A maintained fork of the original `tui-textarea` designed to ensure ongoing compatibility with modern `ratatui` releases (e.g., 0.30+) and recent Rust versions[3][4].

If you are starting a new project or upgrading an existing one, check the [Ratatui Third-Party Widgets Showcase](https://ratatui.rs/third-party-widgets/) to see which version or fork is currently recommended for your specific `ratatui` version[5].

+    #[test]
+    fn name_input_textarea_editing_and_submission() {
+        let mut app = App::new(
+            "Test".into(),
+            "/tmp/Test.toml".into(),
+            UserTheme::default_theme(),
+        );
+
+        app.open_name_input(NameInputPurpose::HostnameRstrip, ".local");
+        assert_eq!(app.name_input_buffer(), ".local");
+
+        // Type additional suffix
+        for c in ",.internal".chars() {
+            app.handle_key(KeyCode::Char(c), crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        }
+        assert_eq!(app.name_input_buffer(), ".local,.internal");
+
+        // Press Backspace
+        app.handle_key(KeyCode::Backspace, crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        assert_eq!(app.name_input_buffer(), ".local,.interna");
+
+        // Submit with Enter
+        app.selected_component = app
+            .theme
+            .components
+            .iter()
+            .position(|c| c.id == ComponentId::Hostname)
+            .unwrap();
+        app.name_input_purpose = NameInputPurpose::HostnameRstrip;
+        app.handle_key(KeyCode::Enter, crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        assert!(!app.name_input_open);
+
+        let rstrip = app.theme.components[app.selected_component]
+            .options
+            .get("rstrip")
+            .and_then(|v| v.as_str());
+        assert_eq!(rstrip, Some(".local,.interna"));
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn icon_picker_search_and_custom_textareas() {
+        let mut app = App::new(
+            "Test".into(),
+            "/tmp/Test.toml".into(),
+            UserTheme::default_theme(),
+        );
+
+        app.open_icon_picker(crate::tui::app::IconPickerPurpose::PlainIcon);
+        assert!(app.icon_picker_open);
+
+        // Search textarea input
+        for c in "rocket".chars() {
+            app.handle_key(KeyCode::Char(c), crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        }
+        assert_eq!(app.icon_picker.search_query(), "rocket");
+
+        // Switch to custom tab
+        app.icon_picker
+            .tab
+            .set(&crate::data::icon_catalog::IconPickerTab::Custom);
+        app.icon_picker.set_custom_buffer("");
+
+        for c in "🚀✨".chars() {
+            app.handle_key(KeyCode::Char(c), crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        }
+        assert_eq!(app.icon_picker.custom_buffer(), "🚀✨");
+
+        // Apply custom icon
+        app.handle_key(KeyCode::Enter, crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        assert_eq!(app.theme.components[0].icon.plain, "🚀✨");
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn color_picker_rgb_textareas_editing() {
+        let mut app = App::new(
+            "Test".into(),
+            "/tmp/Test.toml".into(),
+            UserTheme::default_theme(),
+        );
+
+        app.selected_component = 0;
+        app.selected_field = FieldSelection::TextColor;
+        app.open_color_picker();
+        assert!(app.color_picker_open);
+
+        app.color_picker.mode.set(&crate::tui::app::ColorPickerMode::Rgb);
+        app.color_picker.set_rgb(10, 20, 30);
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.r_val(), 10);
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.g_val(), 20);
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.b_val(), 30);
+
+        // Clear R field and type 255
+        app.color_picker.rgb_focus = 0;
+        app.color_picker.rgb_textareas[0] = ratatui_textarea::TextArea::new(vec![String::new()]);
+        for c in "255".chars() {
+            app.handle_key(KeyCode::Char(c), crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        }
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.r_val(), 255);
+
+        // Move to G field and edit
+        app.handle_key(KeyCode::Down, crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.rgb_focus, 1);
+        app.color_picker.rgb_textareas[1] = ratatui_textarea::TextArea::new(vec![String::new()]);
+        for c in "100".chars() {
+            app.handle_key(KeyCode::Char(c), crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        }
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.g_val(), 100);
+
+        // Submit color
+        app.handle_key(KeyCode::Enter, crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        assert!(!app.color_picker_open);
+        assert_eq!(
+            app.theme.components[0].colors.text,
+            Some(crate::config::types::AnsiColor::Rgb {
+                r: 255,
+                g: 100,
+                b: 30,
+            })
+        );
+    }

+

+        app.color_picker.set_rgb(10, 20, 30);
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.r_val(), 10);

+        app.color_picker.rgb_focus = 0;
+        app.color_picker.rgb_textareas[0] = ratatui_textarea::TextArea::new(vec![String::new()]);

+            app.handle_key(KeyCode::Char(c), crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        }
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.r_val(), 255);
+
+        // Move to G field and edit
+        app.handle_key(KeyCode::Down, crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.rgb_focus, 1);
+        app.color_picker.rgb_textareas[1] = ratatui_textarea::TextArea::new(vec![String::new()]);
+        for c in "100".chars() {
+            app.handle_key(KeyCode::Char(c), crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        }
+        assert_eq!(app.color_picker.g_val(), 100);
+
+        // Submit color
+        app.handle_key(KeyCode::Enter, crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::NONE);
+        assert!(!app.color_picker_open);
+        assert_eq!(
+            app.theme.components[0].colors.text,
+            Some(crate::config::types::AnsiColor::Rgb {
+                r: 255,
+                g: 100,
+                b: 30,
+            })
+        );
+    }
